 33: 0xB05A [0x2B] Offa (ID: 17739818/0x010EB02A) [12416*]:
    → "I finally went and retrieved the letters my friends and I had written to ourselves all those years ago. Can you guess what my letter said?"

 39: 0xB09E [0x2B] Offa (ID: 17739818/0x010EB02A) [12417*]:
    → ""Don't ask me. It's up to you." That's what I wrote."
 40: 0xB0A5 [0x23] WAIT_FOR_DIALOG_INTERACTION
 41: 0xB0A6 [0x52] END_LOAD_SCHEDULER: End scheduler "s100" with entities [EventEntity, EventEntity], work=331*

 44: 0xB0CD [0x2B] Offa (ID: 17739818/0x010EB02A) [12418*]:
    → "All the others had written such beautiful letters, full of the hope we were seeking during the war. "I want to be a valiant soldier." "I want to be a gifted doctor who saves many lives." Everyone else had dreams...everyone but me."

 46: 0xB0D5 [0x2A] GET_REQ_LEVEL(level=5, entity_id=Offa (ID: 17739818/0x010EB02A))

 48: 0xB0E4 [0x2B] Offa (ID: 17739818/0x010EB02A) [12419*]:
    → "I had no hopes or aspirations. I couldn't think of anything to write. So I just put it off and delayed making a decision for as long as possible..."

 52: 0xB10C [0x2B] Offa (ID: 17739818/0x010EB02A) [12420*]:

 54: 0xB114 [0x52] END_LOAD_SCHEDULER: End scheduler "s102" with entities [EventEntity, EventEntity], work=331*

 59: 0xB14F [0x2B] Offa (ID: 17739818/0x010EB02A) [12421*]:
